May I enter you as such, Lord. Hail!

You as such enter me, O Lord. Hail! In That (who is) of thousand branches, in you, O Lord, do I wash myself. Hail!

As waters go downward; as months into the year; so to me may students of sacred knowledge, O Disposer (Dhātṛ), come from all sides. Hail!

[[P276]]

You are a refuge; shine upon me, come to me. (3)

V (Contemplation of four mystic utterances)

Bhūr, Bhuvas, Suvar: verily, these (are) the three utterances (vyāhṛtis).

Of them, verily, the fourth, namely Mahas, Mahācamasya discovered. That (is) Brahman; That is self (ātman); (His) limbs (are) the other deities.

Bhūr, verily, (is) this world; Bhuvas, the mid-region; Suvar, the other world (1); Mahas, the sun; by the sun, indeed, all the worlds are glorified. Bhūr, verily, (is) Fire (Agni); Bhuvas, the wind; Suvar, the sun (Āditya); Mahas, the Moon (Candramas); by Moon, indeed, all the luminaries are glorified. Bhūr, verily, (is) the Ṛks; Bhuvas, the Sāmans; Suvar, the Yajuses (2); Mahas, the Brahman; by Brahman, indeed, all the Vedas are glorified.

Bhūr, verily, (is) the vital breath (prāṇa); Bhuvas, the out-breath (apāna); Suvar, the pervading breath (vyāna); Mahas, the food (anna); by food, indeed, all the vital breaths are glorified.

They, verily, these four (are) fourfold; four, four (are) the utterances (vyāhṛtis).

Who knows these, he knows Brahman; all the gods bring offering to him. (3)

[[P277]]

VI (Relation of the utterances with Brahman)

This space that is within the heart, therein is this Person (Puruṣa), consisting of mind (manomaya), immortal (and) bright.

Within the palate, that which hangs down like a nipple, that is the birthplace of Indra486, where that hair-end splits dividing the skull.

(As) Bhūr he resides in Fire (Agni); (as) Bhuvas in wind (Vāyu); (as) Suvar in the Sun; (as) Mahas in Brahman. He obtains self-leadership (svārājya); he obtains the lord of mind, lord of the voice, lord of the eye, lord of the ear, lord of knowledge; (and) then becomes this Brahman whose body is the space (ākāśa-śarīra), whose soul is the truth (satyātmā), whose vital breath is delight (prāṇārāma), whose mind is bliss (manānanda), who is prosperous with peace (and who is) immortal.487

Thus, O Prācīnayogya, worship.

VII (Brahman as fivefold)

Earth, mid-region, heaven, quarters, intermediate quarters; Fire (Agni), Wind (Vāyu), Sun (Āditya), Moon (Candramas), Stars (Nakṣatras); waters, plants, trees, space (ākāśa) and self (ātman): this much concerning material existence (ādhibhūta).

Now concerning the self (adhyātma): prāṇa, vyāna, apāna, udāna, samāna; eye, ear, mind (manas), speech, touch; skin, flesh, muscle, bone, marrow.

Having ordained this, the seer said: ‘Fivefold (paṅkta), verily, is this all; with the fivefold, indeed, one strengthens the fivefold.’ (1)

[[P278]]

VIII (Contemplation of Om)

Om is Brahman.

Om is this all (world).

Om, verily, this is compliance; and (on uttering) ‘O recite’ they recite. With Om they sing Sāmans. With Om ‘Śaṃs’ they recite the prayers (śastras). With Om the Adhvaryu (priest) utters the encouraging word (pratigara). With Om the Brahman (priest) impels. With Om (one) assents to the oblation to Fire (Agnihotra). ‘Om’ says the Brāhmaṇa about to recite ‘May I obtain Brahman.’ He does obtain the Brahman. (1)

IX (Study and teaching with all other duties)

The settled order (ṛta), as well as study and teaching; the truth, as well as study and teaching; penance, as well as study and teaching; self-control, as well as study and teaching; peace, as well as study and teaching; the (sacrificial) fires, as well as study and teaching; Agnihotra (sacrifice), as well as study and teaching; guests, as well as study and teaching; humanity, as well as study and teaching; offspring, as well as study and teaching; begetting, as well as study and teaching; procreation, as well as study and teaching.

‘The truth’—(says) Satyavacas, the son of Puruṣiṣṭa.

‘Study and teaching only’—says Naka, the son of Mudgala, for that is penance, for that is penance.

X (Ātma-Meditation)

I am the mover of the tree (worldly illusion); (my) fame (is) like the mountain’s peak. I am highly pure like the excellent Immortal in the sun. (I am) shining wealth, (I am) wise, immortal and undecaying. This is Triśaṅku’s recitation of Vedic knowledge. (1)

XI (Exhortation after teaching)

Having taught the Veda, the teacher exhorts the pupils:

Speak the truth, follow the religion.

Do not swerve from study. Having brought the acceptable wealth to the teacher, do not cut off the line of progeny. From truth (you) should not swerve. From religion (you) should not swerve. From welfare (you) should not swerve. From prosperity (you) should not swerve. From study and teaching (you) should not swerve. From the duties to the gods and to the fathers (you) should not swerve. (1)

[[P279]]

Be one to whom mother is god; be one to whom father is god; be one to whom teacher is god; be one to whom guest is god.

What acts are irreproachable, they should be practiced (and) not others.

What are good deeds of ours, they should be practiced by you (and) not others. (2)

Whatever Brāhmaṇas are superior to us, their refreshment should be done by you with a seat.

With faith (śraddhā) should be given (any gift); without faith should not be given. With liberality (śrī) should be given (any gift); with modesty should be given; with fear should be given; with sympathy should be given.

Now, if there occurs any doubt concerning an act or a doubt concerning conduct (3), then, as the Brāhmaṇas there, who are thoughtful, zealous, devoted, not harsh, desirous of religion, may behave in such matters, so should you behave in such matters.

Now, with regard to the accused: as the Brāhmaṇas there, who are thoughtful, zealous, devoted, not harsh, desirous of religion, may behave in such matters, so should you behave in such matters.

This is the direction. This is the advice. This is the mystic doctrine of the Veda. This is the instruction. Thus you should worship. Thus, indeed, (you) should worship. (4)

[[P280]]

XII (Prayer for auspices and peace)

Om. Auspicious for us (may) Mitra (be), auspicious (may) Varuna (be), auspicious for us may Aryaman be; auspicious for us (may) Indra (and) Bṛhaspati (be); auspicious for us (may) wide-strider Viṣṇu (be).488

Salutation to Brahman, salutation to you, O Vayu; you, indeed, are the perceptible Brahman; I declared you, indeed, the perceptible Brahman; I declared (you) settled order, I declared (you) truth. That protected me, That protected the speaker; That protected me, That protected the speaker.

Om. Peace, peace, peace.489

[[P281]]

[[P282]]

VIII

(The Taittiriyopanisad, Brahmananda-Valli)

I (Prayer for good-feeling between teacher and disciple. Knowledge of Brahman)

Harih Om. May (Brahman) protect us both together.490 May (He) nourish us both together. May we both show strength together. May the learning of us both be brilliant. May we not hate each other. Om. Peace, peace, peace.491

Om. The knower of the Brahman attains the Supreme.492

As to that this has been said: Real, Knowledge, Infinite is Brahman; he who knows (That) set down in the cave (of heart) (and) in the highest heaven, obtains all the desires together with the Brahman.

From That, verily, from this Soul the space (ākāśa) was born; from space, wind (vāyu); from wind, fire; from fire, waters; from waters, earth; from earth, plants; from plants, food; from food, man. He, verily, this man consists of food-essence. Thus, indeed, is his head; this, the right side; this, the tail; this, the left side; this, the self; the support.

As to that there is also this verse:

[[P283]]

II (Food as Brahman. From food to breath)

From food, verily, creatures are born, whatever (creatures) dwell on earth. Moreover, by food, surely, they live; and then again they go to the food in the end. For food is, truly, the eldest of beings; therefore, it is called the medicament of all. All food, verily, they obtain, who worship food as Brahman. For food is, truly, the eldest of beings; therefore, it is called the medicament of all. From food are beings born; by food, when born, they grow. It is eaten and it eats beings; thence it is called food.

Then that, verily—than this one consisting of food-essence, there is another self within, consisting of breath (prāṇa); by him this is filled.

He, verily, this one, is just like man. After his human shape, this one (consisting of breath) is like man. Of him the in-breath (prāṇa) is itself head; the diffused breath (vyāna), the right wing; the out-breath (apāna), the left wing; space, the self; earth, the tail, the support.

As to that there is also this verse:

III (Prāṇa as Brahman. From prāṇa to manas)

The gods breathe with breath (prāṇa), as also men and beasts. For breath, verily, is the life-duration of all beings; therefore, it is called the life-duration of all. The whole life-duration do they obtain, who worship breath as Brahman. For breath, verily, is the life-duration of beings; therefore, it is called the life-duration of all. Of that, of the former, this one, verily, is the self embodied.

[[P284]]

Than that, verily—than this one consisting of breath, there is another self within, consisting of mind (manas); by him this is filled.

He, verily, this one, is just like man. After his human shape, this one (consisting of mind) is like man. Of him the yajus itself is the head; ṛk, the right wing; sāman, the left wing; ordinance, the self; the Atharvanas and the Āṅgirasas, the tail, the support.

As to that there is also this verse:

IV (Brahman beyond speech and thought. From mind to knowledge)

Whence words turn back together with the mind without reaching, he who knows that bliss of Brahman fears not at any time. Of that, of the former, verily, this is the self embodied.

Than that, verily—than this one consisting of mind, there is another self within, consisting of knowledge (vijñāna); by him this is filled.

He, verily, this one, is just like man. After his human shape, this one (consisting of knowledge) is like man. Of him, faith itself is the head; settled order (ṛta), the right wing; truth, the left wing; contemplation (yoga), the self; might (mahas), the tail, the support.

As to that there is this verse:

[[P285]]

V (Knowledge as Brahman. From knowledge to bliss)

Knowledge accomplishes sacrifice; and also accomplishes deeds; all gods worship knowledge as Brahman, the Eldest. If (one) understands knowledge as Brahman (and) if from That he does not swerve, he, leaving sin in the body, obtains all desires. Of that, of the former, verily, this is the self embodied.

Than that, verily—than this one consisting of knowledge, there is another self within, consisting of bliss (ānanda); by him this is filled.

He, verily, this one, is just like man. After his human shape, this one consisting of bliss is like man. Of him pleasure (priya) itself is the head; delight (moda), the right wing; great delight (pramoda), the left wing; bliss, the self; Brahman, the tail, the support.

As to that there is also this verse:

VI (Brahman)

Non-existent (asat), verily, he becomes, if he knows Brahman non-existent. If he knows Brahman existent, then (people) know him existent. Of that, of the former, verily, this is the self embodied.

Now next, the appurtenant questions:

Does any one, who knows not, go to the yonder world after departure (from this world)? Or does anyone, who knows, obtain the yonder world after departure (from this world)?

He desired: ‘Let me be many, let me procreate myself.’

[[P286]]

He performed penance; after performing penance he created all this whatsoever. Having created it, into it, indeed, he entered.

Having entered it, he became existent and tyat; defined and undefined; support and non-support; conscious and unconscious; true (real) and false; whatever there is here, that all they call real.

As to that there is also this verse:

VII (Creation)

This (world) was, verily, non-existent in the beginning; thence, verily, existence was produced. That made its soul itself; therefore it is called the well-done (or self-done).

Verily, what, that is well-done (or self-done), that, verily, is essence (rasa). For on getting the essence, truly, (one) becomes blissful.

For who would breathe out (or) who would breathe in, if there were not this bliss in space? For this, certainly, causes bliss. For, when, truly, this (ātman) finds fearlessness as a support in this invisible, bodiless, undefined (and) unsupported, then he has reached fearlessness.

When, however, this (ātman) makes any little difference in this (Brahman), then there is fear for him.

That (Brahman) himself is (the cause of) fear for the knower (of the difference) (who is) not thinker.

As to that there is also this verse:

[[P287]]

VIII (Supreme Brahman and Bliss)

Through fear of Him the wind (vāyu) blows; through fear the sun (sūrya) rises; through fear of Him Fire (Agni) and Indra (and) Death (mṛtyu) as fifth speed.

This is that consideration (mīmāṃsā) of bliss:

Let there be a youth, a good youth (sadā-yuva), studious, most quick, most firm and most vigorous; let this whole earth be full of wealth for him. That is one human bliss.

Such hundred human blisses (1) are one bliss of the human Gandharvas as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten with desires.

Such hundred blisses of the human Gandharvas are one bliss of the divine Gandharvas as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten with desires. Such hundred blisses of the divine Gandharvas are one bliss of the fathers of long-enduring world as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten with desires.

Such hundred blisses of the fathers of the long-enduring world are one bliss of the gods born in the divine world (2) as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten by desires.

Such hundred blisses of the gods born in the divine world are one bliss of the gods divine by work—who go to gods by their work, as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten by the desires.

Such hundred blisses of the gods divine by work are one bliss of gods as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten by the desires.

[[P288]]

Such hundred blisses of gods are one bliss of Indra (3) as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten by the desires.

Such hundred blisses of Indra are one bliss of Bṛhaspati as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten by the desires.

Such hundred blisses of Bṛhaspati are one bliss of Prajāpati as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten by the desires.

Such hundred blisses of Prajāpati are one bliss of Brahman as well as of one well-versed in the Vedas and not smitten by the desires (4).

He who is in the man and who is in the sun—he is one.

He who knowing this, after departing from this world, proceeds on to this self, consisting of food; proceeds on to this self, consisting of breath; proceeds on to this self, consisting of mind; proceeds on to this self, consisting of knowledge; proceeds on to this self, consisting of bliss.

As to that there is also this verse:

IX (Brahman beyond speech and mind)

Whence words turn back together with the mind without reaching, he who knows that bliss of Brahman fears not from any side.

Him, certainly, does not torment (the thought), “Why have I not done good and why have I done sin.”

He who knows thus, regards these two as Ātman. This is the mystic doctrine (upaniṣat).

[[P289]]

May (Brahman) protect us both together. May (He) nourish us both together. May we both show strength together. May the learning of us both be brilliant. May we not hate each other.

Peace, peace, peace.493

[[P290]]

[[P291]]

IX

(The Taittirīya Upaniṣad: Bhṛgu-Valli)494

I (Attainment of the Knower of Brahman)

Harih Om. May (Brahman) protect us both together. May (He) nourish us both together. May we both show strength together. May the learning of us both be brilliant. May we not hate each other. Om. Peace, peace, peace.495

Bhṛgu, verily, the son of Varuṇa, went to his father Varuṇa (and said): “Teach (me) Brahman, Sir.”

He said to him: “Food, breath, eye, ear, mind, speech.”

He said again: “Whence, verily, these beings are born; by whom, when born, they live; to whom they go and merge (thereinto); be desirous to know That; That is Brahman.”

He (Bhṛgu) performed penance. Having performed penance:—

II (Food as Brahman)

He understood food as Brahman. For from food, verily, these beings are born; by food, when born, they live; to food they go and merge (thereinto).

Having understood that, he went again to (his) father Varuṇa (and said): “Teach (me) Brahman, Sir.”

To him he said: “By penance desire to know Brahman; penance is Brahman.”

He performed penance. Having performed penance:—

[[P292]]

III (Prāṇa as Brahman)

He understood breath (prāṇa) as Brahman. For from breath, verily, these beings are born; by breath, when born, they live; to breath they go and merge (thereinto).

Having understood that, he went again to (his) father Varuṇa (and said): “Teach (me) Brahman, Sir.”

To him he said: “By penance desire to know Brahman; penance is Brahman.”

He performed penance. Having performed penance:—

IV (Mind as Brahman)

He understood mind as Brahman. For from mind, verily, these beings are born; by mind, when born, they live; to mind they go and merge (thereinto).

Having understood that, he went again to (his) father Varuṇa (and said): “Teach (me) Brahman, Sir.”

To him he said: “By penance desire to know Brahman; penance is Brahman.”

He performed penance. Having performed penance:—

V (Knowledge as Brahman)

He understood knowledge as Brahman. For from knowledge, verily, these beings are born; by knowledge, when born, they live; to knowledge they go and merge (thereinto).

Having understood that, he went again to (his) father Varuṇa (and said): “Teach (me) Brahman, Sir.”

To him he said: “By penance desire to know Brahman; penance is Brahman.”

He performed penance. Having performed penance:—

[[P293]]

VI (Bliss as Brahman)

He understood bliss (ānanda) as Brahman. For from bliss, verily, these beings are born; by bliss, when born, they live; to bliss they go and merge (thereinto).

This is the knowledge of Bhṛgu, the son of Varuṇa, fixed in the highest heaven.

He who knows this, becomes fixed; becomes possessor of food (and) eater of food; becomes great in progeny, cattle, splendour of sacred knowledge (brahma-varcas) (and) great in fame.

VII (Importance of food)

(One) should not abuse food. That is the duty (vrata).

Breath, verily, is food; body, the eater of food. The body is fixed in breath; breath is fixed in the body. That is, this food is fixed in food.

He who knows this food fixed in food, becomes fixed; becomes possessor of food (and) eater of food; becomes great in progeny, cattle, splendour of sacred knowledge (brahma-varcas) (and) great in fame.

VIII (Importance of food)

(One) should not despise food. That is the duty.

Waters, verily, are food; light, the eater of food. Light is fixed in waters; waters are fixed in light. That is, this food is fixed in food.

He who knows this food fixed in food, becomes fixed; becomes possessor (and) eater of food; becomes great in progeny, cattle, splendour of sacred knowledge (brahma-varcas) (and) great in fame.

IX (Importance of food)

One should make food much. That is the duty.

[[P294]]

The earth, verily, is food; space, the eater of food. Space is fixed in the earth; the earth is fixed in space. That is, this food is fixed in food.

He who knows this food fixed in food, becomes fixed; becomes possessor of food (and) eater of food; becomes great in progeny, cattle, splendour of sacred knowledge (brahma-varcas) (and) great in fame.

X (Food and Knowledge of Brahman)

(One) should not despise anyone at his dwelling. That is the duty.

Therefore, in any way whatsoever, one should obtain much food.

‘I have asked cooked food for this’ they say. This, verily, is the food cooked primarily; (hence) primarily food is cooked for him. This, verily, is the food cooked in the middle; (hence) in the middle food is cooked for him. This, verily, is the food cooked in the end; (hence) in the end food is cooked for him. (1)

He who knows this (he obtains the aforesaid things).

As protection in speech, as acquisition and protection in the in-breath (prāṇa) and the out-breath (apāna), as work in the hands, as motion in the feet, as discharge in the anus: these are the human worships (of Brahman). Now the divine ones: as satisfaction in the rain, as power in lightning, as fame in the cattle, as light in the stars, as procreation, immortality and bliss in the generative organ, as all in space.

(One) should worship That as support; (then he) becomes possessor of the support. (One) should worship That as greatness; (then he) becomes great. (One) should worship That as mind; (then he) becomes honourable. (One) should worship That as salutation; (then) all the desires bow him down. (One) should worship That as Brahman; (then he) becomes realizer of Brahman. (One) should worship That as Brahman’s round-place of destruction; (then) around him die envious rivals (and those) who are unloving foes.

[[P295]]

He who is in the man and who is in the sun—he is one. (4)

He who knows this, after departing from this world, proceeding on to this self, consisting of food; proceeding on to this self, consisting of breath; proceeding on to this self, consisting of mind; proceeding on to this self, consisting of knowledge; proceeding on to this self, consisting of bliss; roaming in these worlds according to his desire, and having food according to his desire, lives singing this sāman:—

Oh, oh, oh!

I am food, I am food, I am food! I am eater of food, I am eater of food, I am eater of food! I am maker of fame, I am maker of fame, I am maker of fame! I am the first-born of the settled order (ṛta), before the gods, (and I am) the navel of immortality. Who gives me, he protects me. I am food; I eat the eater of food. I have conquered all the world. I am light like (that of) sun.

He who knows thus—he obtains the aforesaid things. This is the mystic doctrine (upaniṣat).

Om. May (Brahman) protect us both together. May (He) nourish us both together. May we both show strength together. May the learning of us both be brilliant. May we not hate each other. Om. Peace, peace, peace.

[[P296]]

X

[[P297]]

I

Hariḥ Om.

May He protect us both together; may he nourish us both together; may we show vigour together; may our learning be brilliant; may we not hate each other.

Om Peace!

Peace!

Peace!

1. Across the water, in the world, at the back of the heaven, greater than the great, entered the lights by shining, in the womb (of the world-creation) Prajapati moves.

2. In whom this all is born and in whom this all vanishes; in whom all the gods live; that is the being and that is what is to be; this all is in that immortal and supreme heaven.

3. By whom has been pervaded the sky, the heaven, the earth, by whom the sun shines by brilliance and splendour; whom the poets see woven in the midst of the sea (world); in that supreme and immortal one are the subjects.

4. From whom the birth of the world has been born, who created by the water (matters) all the beings in the earth; who entered all the plants, men, cattle, moving and immoving creatures.

5. There is nothing better than this, which is better than the good, greater than the great; which is one, unknown, infinite, all, ancient and above the darkness.

The Narayana or the Yajniki Upanisad.496

[[P298]]

6. To that, indeed, they say Ṛta (Settled Order), truth; that is the best Brahman of the poets; that is the stored-up sacrifices; as the navel that nourishes variously all that are born or that are still to be born.

7. That is, verily, the fire; that is the wind, that is the sun, that is also the moon, that is the shining nectar, that is Brahman, that is the waters, that is Prajapati.

8. All the nimeṣas, kalās, kāṣṭhās, days and nights (measuring parts of the time) all these were born of the Shining Person; half-months, months, seasons were also made of Him; He milked both the waters, these heaven and the earth and also the mid-region and the heaven.

9. None holds Him above, sidewise or in the middle; none is able to hold Him; hence His name is ‘Great Fame’. His form does not stand to be seen; none sees Him by the eye; by the heart, by the thinking, by the mind He is found; who know Him, they become immortal.

10. “Hiranyagarbha, born of the waters” Mantras (of Prapāṭhaka III, Anuvāka XIII), these eight.497

11. This God is, verily, in all the quarters; He was born before and even he is in the womb; He is being born; He is to be born; He is the Head of all the limbs; He stands having his face all round. He has eyes all round, mouth all round, hands all round, feet all round; He bends well by hands and falling ones (rules by the religion etc.); He is One producing the heaven and the earth.

12. Learned Vena, the learned Gandharva observing all the worlds and that, where all the worlds rest as in a nest, in which this all is born and in which this all vanishes; in which this all is woven, which is pervading in the subjects taught that nectar and the three feet (rising, dreaming and sleeping conditions) which are put in the minds; he, who knows that, becomes even the father of his producer (father).

[[P299]]

13. That (god) is our brother, father, disposer; He knows all the places, where, in the third sphere, all the gods, drinking the nectar obtained their places (who know Him) they pervade the heaven and the earth soon, the worlds, the quarter and the paradise; who knows the line of the Ṛta (Settled Order = God) discriminatingly, he becomes That among the subjects.

14. Prajapati, the first born of the Ṛta (God) pervading the worlds, pervading the beings, pervading all the great quarters and the sub-quarters obtained the Self by himself.

15. I have obtained the lord of the assembly, the wonderful one, dear to Indra, worth desiring, winning and the intellect.

16. O fire, be shining up destroying my poverty; bring for me cattle and life from all the directions.

17. O fire, do not kill our cow, horse, man and the moving ones; O fire, come forgetting our sins; meet us with prosperity.

18. May we know and meditate the form of the person, having thousand eyes, the great God (Mahadeva); may that Rudra impel us.

19. May we know and meditate the form of the person, the great god (Mahadeva); may that Rudra impel us.

20. May we know and meditate the form of Vakratunda; may that Danti impel us.

21. May we know and meditate the form of the person, having golden feathers; may that Garuda impel us.

[[P300]]

22. May we know and meditate the form of Katyayana, Kanyakumari; may that Durgi impel us.

23. May we know and meditate the form of Narayana Vasudeva; may that Visnu impel us.

24. May the Durva-grass, the goddess, better than thousands, having hundred roots, the destroyer of the bad dreams destroy our all sin.498

25. O earth, trodden by the horse, trodden by the chariot, trodden by Visnu protect us at every step being put on my head (by me).

26. You have been raised up by the Black Boar, having hundred arms; you are the earth, the cow, the holder (of the plants etc.) and the holder of the beings.

27. O earth, destroy my sin and whatever bad deed I have done; may I live hundred autumns, my sins being destroyed by you.

28. O earth, give me nourishing; everything stands firm in you.499

29. I invite that prosperity (earth), which has the doors of the perfume, non-injurable, always satisfied, full of dung, the lord of all the beings.

30. May the waters and the plants be good friends to us; may they become bad friends to them, who hate us and whom we hate.500

[[P301]]

31. I obtain Varuna, having the gold born (crown); give me entering place being prayed; may Indra, Varuna, Brhaspati, Savitr purify again and again whatever I have eaten of wicked ones, whatever I have taken from the sinful ones, whatever sin has been done by me by mind, speech or work.

32. Salutation to the fire, full of the water, salutation to Indra, salutation to Varuna, salutation to Varuni (the wife of Varuna), salutation to the waters.501

33. What is cruel, impure and peaceless of the waters; may that go away.502

34. May Varuna take away by his hand whatever sin I have done by eating much, drinking much, taking gifts cruelly; may that I, becoming sinless, without dust, free, free from the fault, ascending the back of the heaven go to the eternal Brahman.503

35. Hear this (my prayer) and be gathered O Ganges, O Yamuna, Sarasvati, O Sutudri with Parusani, Asikni, O Marudvrdha with Vitasta, O Arjikiya with Susoma.504

36. Ṛta (settled order) and truth was born of the shining One after austerity; then the night was born, then the various seas; from the various seas the year, the lord of the all moments was born creating the days and the nights; the disposer made the sun and the moon as before and also the heaven, the earth, the mid-region and the paradise.505

[[P302]]

37. Whatever is our sin in the earth, in the mid-region and in the places between the heaven and the earth; may the waters and sin-destroying Varuna purify that. This (Varuna) is the protector of the world among the beings; this is the worlds of the pious ones; this is the golden world of the death; this is the golden place of the heaven and the earth depending on the paradise; may he sharpen my paradise.506

38. The wet (water) is shining; I am the light. The light is shining; I am Brahman. Whoever am I, I am Brahman. I am Brahman, I am. I am I; I sacrifice for me.507

39. Performer of improper deeds, one who has split his semen virile, the thief, the killer of the embryo, one who sleeps on the bed of his teacher (these are the great sinners); Varuna, the lord of the waters is the destroyer of the sins; one becomes released from that sin.508

40. (Though you can) cause us, the sinners to weep; but may the seers purify, may Vasus purify, may Varuna purify, may the destroyer of the sins purify.509

41. The sea, pervaded all in the beginning creating the beings according to their past deeds; the king of the world (God), the sprinkling and impelling one, delighting one, the great Soma increased himself on the pious, great and non-destroyable peak (of the heart).510

42. We press Soma for Fire (Jatavedas); his knowledge burns those, who wish to be our enemies; he makes us across all the difficulties and sins like the crossing of the sea by a boat.511

[[P303]]

43. I obtain the shelter of the goddess Durga, the fire-coloured one, shining by the austerity, brilliant, standing in the fruits of the works; O good-crossing one, salutation to you, who make across.

44. O fire, worth praising, make us across all the difficulties by protections; may the village be vast, much broad for us; be wealful for our sons and grandsons.

45. O fire, destroyer of the sorrows, you make us across all the sins as one crosses the sea by the boat; O fire, like Atri, become the waking protector of our bodies uttering (protective Mantras) by mind.

46. We sacrifice from the high place of seat for the fire, the winner of the armies, the toleratory one and the fierceful one; that fire, excusing our sins makes us cross all the difficulties and the sins.

47. You are worth worshipping in the sacrifices, you extend the comfort, you sit as donator, sacrificer and one worth praising; make your body pleased (by our oblations); and grant us good fortune all round.

II

1. Bhuh! Hail for the fire and for the earth! Bhuvah! Hail for the wind and for the mid-region! Suvah! Hail for the sun and for the heaven! Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah! for the quarters. Salutation for the gods! Hail for the moon and Svadha (Hail) for the fathers! Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, Om!512

[[P304]]

III

1. Bhuh, the food, Hail for the fire and for the earth! Bhuvah, the food, Hail for the wind and for the mid-region! Suvah, the food, Hail for the sun and for the heaven! Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, the food, Hail for the moon and for the quarters! Salutation to the gods; Svadha (Hail) for the fathers, Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, the food, Om!513

IV

1. Bhuh, Hail for the fire, for the earth and for the great! Bhuvah, Hail for the wind, for the mid-region and for the great! Suvah, Hail for the sun, for the heaven and for the great! Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, Hail for the moon, for the stars, for the quarters and for the great! Salutation to the gods; Svadha (Hail) for the fathers! Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, Mahah, om!514

V

1. O fire, protect us from the sin, Hail! Protect us for knowledge of all, Hail! O Vibhāvasu (having the shining wealth), protect us for the sacrifice, Hail! O hundred-powered one, protect, protect all!515

[[P305]]

VI

1. Who is the bull of the metres, having all forms, who has entered the metres by the metres, who is the power of the sages; that oldest Indra taught the Upanisad (mystic knowledge) for the (knowing) senses of the seers. Salutation to the gods, Svadha (Hail) for the fathers! Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, Om.516

VII

1. Salutation to Brahman; may holding power be with me; may not be missing; may we become the holder; may you not destroy the hearing of that who praises you.517

VIII

1. Ṛta (settled order) is the austerity; truth is the austerity; peace is the austerity, donation is the austerity; the sacrifice is the austerity; worship Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah; this is the austerity.518

[[P306]]

IX

1. As the perfume of a well flowerful tree blows from distance, similarly the perfume of the pious deed blows from distance. As on the hanging sword upon a well (one) thinks “if I go on the sword I shall become in trouble or even fall in the well”, similarly one should hate the sin.

X

1. The Self (Atman), less than an atom, greater than the great has been put in the cave (heart) of this being, by the delight of the disposer one, who is sorrowless, sees that lord, the greatness, which has no desires.

2. Seven breaths are born of him; seven rays, fuels and seven tongues (seven flames of the fire), these seven worlds in which the beings move all these sevenfold ones are established by that soul, put in the cave (heart).

3. All the seas and the mountains are born of him; various formed rivers flow from that; from him came forth all the plants and the elixirs, connected with which this inner soul lives.

4. That Atman (Self) is Brahman among the gods, knowledge (of words) among the poets, the seer among the learned ones (Vipras); buffalo among the animals, falcon among the vultures, cutting instrument among the woods; that (Atman) as Soma wins all purifying (objects) making utterance (of Mantras).

[[P307]]

5. One unborn (Atman) pleasantly enjoys the unborn (Nature), having red, white and black forms (Rajas, Sattva and Tamas qualities) and generating many offsprings of the same form; and the next unborn (Atman) leaves that (Nature) after enjoyment.

6. The swan (is) seated in purity; Vasu (bright one), seated in the mid-region; the Hotr-priest, seated at the altar; the guest, seated in the house; (that is) seated in the men, seated in the highest, seated in the settled order (and) seated in the firmament.

7. From whom all have been born, except whom there is no other, who entered all the worlds; that Prajapati, being concordant with progeny, is accompanied with three lights (worlds) having sixteen (kalās).

8. We invoke the good disposer, impelling one and observer of the men; he gives us plenty of wealth.

9. O god Savitr, grant us today fortune with progeny and destroy the effect of bad dreams.

10. O god Savitr, destroy all the sins; what is auspicious, bring that for me.

11. May the winds be honeyful for us, who wish the settled order; may the rivers pour honey; may the plants be honeyful.

12. May honey be for us at night and dawn; may the earthly world be honeyful; may the heaven, our father, be honeyful.

13. May the vegetable be honeyful for us; may the sun be honeyful; may the cows be honeyful.

14. (The sacrificers) sprinkled ghee (on the fire); ghee is the cause of this; this depends upon ghee, ghee is its home; O bull (fire), invoke according to our oblations, please and carry the offering (to the gods).

[[P308]]

15. The sweet wave (sun) has risen from the sea (God); with the rays he obtains the nectar; what is the mysterious name of the ghee that is the tongue of the gods and navel of the nectar.

16. May we declare about the name of ghee and in this sacrifice may we hold that also by the salutations; may Brahma hear nearly being praised; four-horned white one (Om, having a, u, m and the sound) has omitted (taught) this (knowledge).

17. Four are his horns, three are the feet, two are the heads and seven are the hands; the bull, bound thricely, roars; the mighty god has entered the mortals.519

  • The sacrifice: Four Vedas, Three Savanas, Two (Brahmodana and Pravargya), Seven metres, Three bindings (Mantra, Brāhmaṇa & Kalpa).
  • The sun: Four quarters, Three Vedas, Two (day and night), Seven rays, Three bindings (fire, lightning and sun).
  • The inspired word: Four (Nama, Akhyāta, Upasarga, Nipāta), Three tenses, Two (Subanta & Tiṅanta), Seven rays, Three bindings (at heart, throat and head).

18. The gods discovered that ghee laid down in the cow in triple shape (milk, curd and ghee), concealed by Panis (demons); Indra produced one shape, the sun another; the third by their own power.

[[P309]]

19. May that god join us with the auspicious memory, who is more than all, Rudra, the great seer, who was first and before the gods and who saw Hiranyagarbha while being born.

20. Than whom there is none better or worse, less or greater, who stands firm in heaven like a tree; by that person this all is full.

21. Neither by deed, nor by offspring nor by wealth, but by renunciation one obtains the nectar, which is shining, laid down in the cave above the heaven, where the sages enter.

22. By the deciding knowledge of the Vedanta and Vijnana (great knowledge), by joining Samnyasa (fourth stage of life, asceticism) whose hearts become pure, those sages, in the world of Brahman, in the last time become liberated by immortality.

23. Which is this little sinless lotus, being the good home, in the middle of the Pura (body), in that little one there is also ether, sorrowless; in the midst of that which lives, that is to be worshipped.

24. Which accent (Om) has been spoken in the beginning of the Veda and which is firm in the end of the Veda, then that (Om), being mingled in the Nature, who is greater, he is the great lord (Mahesvara).

XI

1. (All beings are dependant) upon the god having hundred heads, all over eyes, producer of all propitiation, all, Narayana, immortal and great lord.

2. Purusa (Person) is all this; all beings are dependant upon him, who is better than all, eternal, all, Narayana and Hari.

3. (All beings are dependant) upon the lord of all, Atman, lord, eternal, auspicious, non-movable, Narayana, great knowledge, Atman of all, having great home (Parayana).

[[P310]]

4. Narayana is great Brahman; Narayana is great essence; Narayana is great light, Narayana is great Atman.

5. Whatever world is seen or heard, Narayana stands pervading that all inwardly and outwardly.

6. (Worship that) infinite, non-decreasing, poet, (living) in the middle of the sea, producing all propitiation, like the sheath of the lotus, the heart having its mouth belowward.

7. Which stands below the gland of the throat, above the navel, crossing the distance of twelve fingers, that should be known as the heart, the great home of the all (world).

8. Which hangs by the nerves like the unblown lotus; in the middle of that there is a minute hole; in that everything has been established.

9. In the middle of that is that great fire, having all rays, all round mouth, the first eater, the distributer of the food.

10. That heats his own body from the foot to the head; in the middle of that there is the flame of the fire, like an atom and having its face above.

11. That is shining like the line of the lightning among the blue clouds, that is least like the tail of the Nivara corn, yellow, shining and the simile for an atom.

12. In the middle of that flame the God resides; He is Brahma, He is Siva, He is Indra, He is Aksara (non-destroyable) the Great Self-King.

XII

1. Salutation to Ṛta (settled order), truth, great Brahma, Purusa, yellow and black, having semen above, having irregular eye and having all forms.

[[P311]]

XIII

1. That (God) is, verily, this sun, heating this orb; in that there are those Ṛcas, that is the world of the Ṛcas; and what are the rays shining in this orb, those are the Samans; that is the world of the Samans; and which is the Person in the ray of the orb that is the Yajus-Mantra, that is the orb of the Yajuses; that is the world of the Yajuses; that is this three knowledge shining there, who is this golden Person shining in the middle of the sun.

XIV

1. The sun is, verily, the brilliance, vigour, power, fame, eye, ear, soul, mind, anger, thinking, death, truth, friend, wind, ether, breath, protector of the world, Ka (Prajapati), Kim (everything), Kam (comfort), true, food, life, immortal, living being, all, most comfortable, self-born, Prajapati. The year is that sun. Who is this Person, that is the lord of the beings.

2. He obtains the company and the common world to Brahman and also obtains the company, equal lordship and the common world to these gods.

3. This is the Upanisat (mystic knowledge).

XV

1. The shining sun, Aditya, Om. For that Aditya one performs worship, austerity, truth, he sprinkles honey. That is Brahman, that the water (rain) and the water (of ocean etc.), that is the light, elixir, nectar, Brahman, Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, Om.

[[P312]]

XVI

1. All is Rudra; may salutation be to that Rudra; Rudra is Purusa (Person) the mighty being: salutation, salutation (to him); whatever is all, matter, world, wonderful, various born, to be born; that all is this Rudra; may salutation be to that Rudra.

XVII

1. We speak most wealful speeches to good, good-headed mostly sprinkling, vigorous and heartful Rudra; all this is Rudra; may salutation be to that Rudra.

XVIII

1. Salutation to (Rudra), gold-armed one, lord of the gold, husband of Ambika, husband of Uma; salutation, salutation.

XIX

1. Whose Agnihotra-havani (sacrificial instrument of wood) is made of Vikankata tree, his oblations are firm; they become firm for him, for making him firm.

XX

1. “Make strength” - these five (Mantras of T.S. 1.2.14 should be uttered).

[[P313]]

XXI

1. Aditi is the gods, Gandharvas, men, fathers, Asuras, mother of these beings, Medini (earth, consisting of the fat), great earth, Savitri, Gayatri, Jagati, vast earth, many, all, material, most comfortable, body; that is the true and the immortal, such says Vasistha.

XXII

1. The waters are, verily, all this; the waters are all the beings; the waters are the breaths; the waters are the animals; the waters are the food; the waters are the nectar; the waters are the Samrat, Virat and Svarat; the waters are the metres; the waters are the Yajuses; the waters are the truth; the waters are all the gods; the waters are Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, Om.

XXIII

1. May the waters purify the earth, may the purified earth purify me; may Brahmanaspati purify; may purified Brahma purify me; may the waters purify that all, whatever left food or the non-eatable food (I have eaten) or whatever misdeed has been done by me and whatever gift (I have taken) from the wicked ones.

[[P314]]

XXIV

1. May the fire, the anger and the lord of the anger protect me from the sins of the anger; may the day destroy that sin which I have done in the day by mind, speech, hands, feet and generating organ, and whatever sin is in me; this I sacrifice myself in the seat of immortality, in the truth, in the light, Hail!

XXV

1. May the sun, the anger and the lord of the anger protect me from the sins of the anger; may the night destroy that sin, which I have done in the night by mind, speech, hands, feet and generating organ, and whatever sin is in me; this I sacrifice myself in the seat of the immortality, in the sun and in the light; Hail!

XXVI

1. May Gayatri, the boon-giving goddess, the mother of the metres come to me, the immortal one like Brahman; may that speak then Brahman (sacred knowledge) to us; you are the brightness, you are the tolerable one, you are the power, you are the shining, you are the nature of the places of the gods, you are all, you are all, all life, winner, Om, I invoke Gayatri.

XXVII

1. Om Bhuh, Om Bhuvah, Om Suvah, Om Mahah, Om Janah, Om austerity, Om truth; Om may we meditate that ray worth choosing of the impelling god (Savitr); which may impell my wisdom, Om, the waters, the light, the elixir, the nectar, Brahman, Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, Om!

[[P315]]

XXVIII

1. Om Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, Mahah, Janah, austerity, truth, that is Brahman, that is the waters, the waters, the light, the elixir, the nectar, Brahman, Bhuh, Bhuvah, Suvah, Om.

XXIX

1. Om, that is Brahman! Om, that is wind! Om, that is Atman! Om, that is all! Om, that is the Pura (body), salutation!

XXX

1. Go as a goddess (O Gayatri) at the supreme peak, at the head of the mountain comfortably being assented by the Brahmanas.

XXXI

1. Om, you move in the beings, in the cave (heart), in all the forms; you are the sacrifice, you are Visnu, you are Vasat-making, you are Rudra, you are Brahma, you are Prajapati.

[[P316]]

XXXII

1. You are the bed of the nectar.520

XXXIII

1. I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Prana-breath; Hail for the Prana! I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Apana-breath; Hail for the Apana! I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Vyana-breath; Hail for the Vyana! I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Udana-breath; Hail for the Udana! I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Samana-breath; Hail for the Samana: My soul is in Brahman for immortality.521

XXXIV

1. I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Prana-breath; enter me being auspicious for not heating; Hail for the Prana. I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Apana-breath; enter me being auspicious for not heating; Hail for the Apana! I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Vyana-breath; enter me being auspicious for not heating; Hail for the Vyana! I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Udana-breath; enter me being auspicious for not heating; Hail for the Udana! I sacrifice nectar sitting in the Samana-breath; enter me being auspicious for not heating; Hail for the Samana! My soul is in Brahman for immortality.522

[[P317]]

XXXV

1. You are the cover of the nectar.523

XXXVI

1. The nectar has been sacrificed sitting in the faith, in the Prana-breath; increase the Prana by the food; the nectar has been sacrificed sitting in the Apana; increase the Apana by the food. The nectar has been sacrificed sitting in the Vyana; increase the Vyana by the food. The nectar has been sacrificed sitting in the Udana; increase the Udana by the food. The nectar has been sacrificed sitting in the Samana; increase the Samana by the food. My soul is in the Brahman for immortality.

XXXVII

1. You are the gland of the breaths, as trouble-destroying Rudra enter me.

XXXVIII

1. The Purusa (Person) is of the length of the thumb; that is put in the thumb (the little piece of the heart); may that eater of all, the lord of all the world, the pervading one become pleased.524

[[P318]]

XXXIX

1. May the intellect, the goddess come to us being pleased; pervading all, auspicious and pleasant-minded. May we, having good sons, being accompanied with you (intellect), though speaking the bad words (not of Vedas) proclaim the great (Brahman), being praised in the assemblies.

2. O goddess, one becomes the seer being accompanied with you; one becomes Brahman and also full of prosperity being accompanied with you; one obtains the wonderful wealth; O Intellect, be accompanied with me taking wealth.

XL

1. May Indra grant us intellect, may the goddess Sarasvati grant us intellect; may Asvines, both gods, having the garland of the lotus-flowers grant us intellect.

XLI

1. May that good-perfuming intellect be accompanied with me, which is in the Apsarases as intellect, in the Gandharvas as the mind, which is the divine intellect and which is the manly intellect.

XLII

1. May that intellect be accompanied with me which is good-perfuming, all-formed, gold-coloured, moving, obtainable, full of vigour, pleasing with the milk and of good appearance.525

[[P319]]

XLIII

1. I obtain the new-born one; salutation to the new-born one; do not make me for taking birth again and again, but make me to cross the birth; salutation to the source of the world.

XLIV

1. Hail for Vamadeva, salutation for the oldest, salutation for the best, salutation for Rudra, salutation for Kala (time); salutation for Kalavikarana; salutation to Balavikarana, salutation to Balapramathane (power-violating one), salutation to Sarvabhutadamana (one who perishes all the beings); salutation to Manonmana (mind-raising one).

XLV

1. O Rudra, may salutation be to your all forms, what are not cruel, what are cruel, what are most cruel; O Sarva (Rudra) salutation to you from all round.

XLVI

1. May we know and meditate the form of Purusa, Mahadeva; may that Rudra impell us.

[[P320]]

XLVII

1. May that Sadasiva (Rudra) be auspicious to us, who is the master of all the learnings, the lord of all the beings, Brahman, the great lord, of Brahman, Brahma.

XLVIII

1. May Brahman come to me, may the honey come to me, may Brahman, verily, the honey come to me. O Soma, what are your offspring, I am one child among those; O destroyer of the bad dreams, destroy (the trouble); O Soma, what are your breaths I sacrifice them.

2. One should give this Trisuparna-Mantra to the Brahmana even being not asked; these destroy the sin of the killing of a Brahmana; Brahmanas, who read the Trisuparna Mantra, they obtain soma and purify the line of thousand (Brahmanas eating together), Om.

XLIX

1. Brahman (is obtained) by the intellect; the honey, by the intellect; Brahman, verily, the honey, by the intellect. O god Savitr, impell today for us prosperity with offspring; destroy away the effect of the bad dream. O god Savitr, destroy all the sins away; bring that to me, which is auspicious.526 May the wind be honeyful for us, who wish the settled order, may the rivers flow being honeyful for us; may the plants be honeyful for us; may the honey be for us at the night and at the dawn; may the earthly region be honeyful to us; may the heaven, our father be honeyful for us; may the vegetable be full of honey for us; may the sun be full of honey, may the cows be honeyful for us.

[[P321]]

2. They who give this Trisuparna Mantra to Brahmana, being not asked for that, they destroy the sin of the killing of the embryo; Brahmanas, who read the Trisuparna Mantra, they obtain Soma and purify the line of thousand (Brahmanas eating together), Om.

L

1. Brahman is full of sacrifice; the honey is full of the sacrifice; Brahman, verily, the honey is full of the sacrifice. That Atman (Self) is Brahman among the gods, knowledge of word among the poets, the seer among the learned ones, buffalo among the animals, falcon among the vultures, cutting instrument among the woods; that (Atman) as Soma wins all purifying (objects) making utterence (of the Mantras). That (is) the swan seated in purity, the bright one seated in the mid-region, the Hotr-priest seated at the altar, the guest seated in the house, seated among men, seated in the highest, seated in the settled order, seated in the firmament, born of the waters, born of the cows, born of the mountain, the great settled order.

2. They, who give this Trisuparna Mantra to Brahmana, they destroy the sin of killing the brave (son). Brahmanas, who read the Trisuparna Mantra, they obtain Soma and purify the line of thousand (Brahmanas eating together), Om.

[[P322]]

LI

1. May my Prana, Apana, Vyana, Udana and Samana breaths be purified, may I become the light, dustless and sinless; Hail!

LII

1. May my speech, mind, eye, ear, tongue, nose, semen, wisdom, purpose and will be purified; may I become the light, dustless and sinless; Hail!

LIII

1. May my head, hand, foot, side, back, thigh, generating organ, urine-organ and the anus be purified; may I become the light, dustless and sinless; Hail!

LIV

1. May my skin, leather, flesh, blood, feet, marrow, sinew and bone be purified; may I become the light, dustless and sinless; Hail!

LV

1. May my word, touch, form, taste and perfume be purified; may I become the light, dustless and sinless; Hail!527

[[P323]]

LVI

1. May my earth, water, light, wind and ether be purified; may I become the light, dustless and sinless; Hail!

LVII

1. May my sheaths of food, breath, mind, knowledge and bliss be purified; may I become the light, dustless and sinless; Hail!

LVIII

1. For the pervading one Hail!

LIX

1. For the desirous of doing Hail!

LX

1. O Purusa (Person), stand up, stand up; O green, red, yellow-eyed (Purusa), give, give (purification) as the giver; may (all) be purified; may I become the light, dustless and sinless; Hail!

LXI

1. Hail! This.

[[P324]]

LXII

1. The truth is the supreme, the supreme is the truth; by the truth they do not fall from the heavenly world at any time; the truth is related to the sages, therefore, they enjoy in the truth.

2. The austerity: there is no better austerity than the fast; which is the great austerity, that is not worth tolerating, that is non-injurable; therefore, they enjoy in the austerity.

3. The self-restraint: therefore, the Brahmacarins (the students of the sacred knowledge), as a rule enjoy in the self-restraint.

4. The place: therefore, the seers enjoy in the peace in the forest.

5. All the beings praise the giving; there is nothing more difficult than the giving; therefore, they enjoy in the giving.

6. The religion: by the religion all this has been held; there is nothing more difficult than the religion; therefore, they enjoy in the religion.

7. Many people (believe in) generating; therefore, they are born in plenty; therefore, many people enjoy in the generating.

8. One says the fire (to be great); therefore, the fires should be heaped.

9. One says the Agnihotra (to be great); therefore, they enjoy in the Agnihotra.

10. The sacrifice: because the gods went to the heavens by the sacrifices, therefore, they enjoy in the sacrifices.

11. The learned ones (believe in) the praise by the mind; therefore, the learned ones enjoy in the praise by the mind.

[[P325]]

12. Brahma (believes in) the renunciation; Brahma is the supreme, the supreme is Brahma; Brahma finalized all the lower austerities in the renunciation.

13. He, who knows thus, this is the Upanisat (mystic knowledge for him).

LXIII

1. Aruni, the son of Prajapati and Suparna, went to his father Prajapati asking what that prosperous one says to be the supreme. He (Prajapati) said this to him:

2. The wind blows by the truth; by the truth the sun shines in the heaven; the truth is the firm-standing of the speech, everything is established in the truth; therefore, they say the truth to be the supreme.

3. By the austerity the gods obtained their position of the gods; by the austerity, by the austerity we destroy our enemies; everything is established in the austerity; therefore, they say the austerity to be supreme.

4. By the self-restraint the self-restraining ones destroy their sins; by the self-restraint the Brahmacarins (students of the sacred knowledge) went to the heaven; the self-restraint is non-injurable among the men; everything is established in the self-restraint; therefore, they say the self-restraint to be the supreme.

5. By the peace the peaceful ones conduct the propitiation; the seers obtained the heaven by the peace; the peace is not to be injured among the men; everything is established in the peace; therefore, they say the peace to be the supreme.

[[P326]]

6. The giving is the best Daksina (sacrificial fee); in the world all the people depend upon the giver; by the giving they destroyed the enemies; by the giving their haters become friends; everything is established in the giving; therefore, they say the giving to be the supreme.

7. The religion is the firm-standing of all the world, the subjects go to the religious one; by the religion he destroys the sins; everything is established in the religion; therefore, they say the religion to be the supreme.

8. The generating is the firm-standing in the world; one spreading the line of the progeny well, becomes debtless among the fathers; that is the debtlessness of that; therefore, they say the generating to be the supreme.

9. The fires are, verily, the three knowledges and the Devayana way (leading to the gods); the household fire is the Rc, earth and Rathantara Saman; the Anvaharyapacana fire is the Yajus, the mid-region and Vamadevya Saman; the Ahavaniya fire is the Saman and the heavenly world and the Brhat Saman; therefore, they say the fire to be the supreme.

10. The Agnihotra-sacrifice of the morning and the evening is the price of the house; well sacrificing and the well offering is the beginning of the sacrificial powers and the light of the heavenly world; therefore, they say the Agnihotra to be the supreme.

11. The sacrifice: by the sacrifice the gods went to the heaven; by the sacrifice they destroyed the Asuras; by the sacrifice the haters become the friends; everything is established in the sacrifice; therefore, they say the sacrifice to be supreme.

[[P327]]

12. The praise by the mind is pure and related to Prajapati; by the praise of the mind one sees the good in his mind; the sages created the beings related to the mind; everything is established in the mind; therefore, they say the mind to be the supreme.

13. The thinkers say Brahma as (to be) the renunciation.

14. Brahma is all, most comfortful, self-born, Prajapati and the year.

15. The year is that sun; who is this Purusa (Person) in the sun, that is Paramesthi Brahma, Atman (Self).

16. By what rays the sun shines, by those Parjanya rains; by Parjanya the plants and the vegetables are born; by the plants and the vegetables comes the food, by the food come the breaths, by the breaths the vigour comes, by the vigour comes the austerity, by the austerity comes the faith; by the faith comes the intellect, by the intellect comes the thinking, by the thinking comes the mind, by the mind comes the peace, by the peace comes the heart, by the heart comes the memory, by the memory comes knowledge, by knowledge knows the self; therefore, by giving the food one gives all these; by the food come the breaths of the beings, by the breaths comes the mind, by the mind comes knowledge and by knowledge comes the bliss, Brahman, the cause of the world.

17. That, verily, this Purusa (Person) is fivefold (made of five matters), of five selves, by whom all this earth, mid-region, heaven, quarters and sub-quarters have been pervaded; he is, verily, all this world; he is the born one and he is to be born; he is known by the desire of knowing, he is born of the settled order, most wealthy, the faith, the truth, mighty and above the darkness.

[[P328]]

18. O learned one knowing that as such, do not go to the death.

19. Therefore, the renunciation has been called the supreme among the austerities.

20. O Brahman, you are the impeller of the brightness; you are the joiner in the breath; you are the creator of the world; you are the giver of the brilliance; you are the giver of the shining to the sun; you are the giver of the shining to the moon; you have been taken in the ladle (for the sacrifice); I (meditate) you for the might.

21. By Om one should meditate.

22. He, who knows thus, obtains the greatness of Brahman; therefore, there is the greatness of Brahman.

LXIV

1. Of his sacrifice, who knows thus, Atman is the sacrificer, the faith is the wife, the body is the fuel, the chest is the altar, the little hairs of the body are Barhi-grass, the Veda is the flame, heart is the stake, the desire is the ghee, the anger is the animal, the austerity is the fire, peace-making is the Daksina (sacrificial fee), the speech is the Hotr-priest, the breath is the Udgatr-priest, the eye is the Adhvaryu-priest, the mind is the Brahma-priest, the ear is the Agnidhra-priest.

2. Holding-time without the meal is the consecration; what he eats that is the oblation; what he drinks that is his Soma-drinking; what he enjoys that is the Upasada; whatever he moves, sits or stands, that is the Pravargya; what is the face that is the Ahavaniya fire; what is the uttering that is the offering; what is his knowledge that is his sacrificing; whatever he eats at the evening and the morning, that is the fuel; what are the morning, the noon and the evening, they are the Savanas (pressings of Soma).

[[P329]]

3. What are the days and the nights, they are the Darsa and the Purnamasa sacrifices; what are the half-months and the months, they are the Caturmasyas; what are the seasons, they are the Pasubandhas; what are the years and the cycles of the years, the groups of the days, they are the Sarva-Vedas; verily, this is the Satra (session of the sacrifice); what is the death, that is the Avabhṛtha (the bathing after the sacrifice).

4. This is his Agnihotra-Satra (session of that sacrifice) up to his old age and the death. He, who knowing thus, dies in the Uttarayana (the period of the Sun’s progress to the north), goes to the accompanying of the sun after going to the greatness of the gods. And he, who dies in the Daksinayana (the period of the sun’s progress to the south), goes to the accompanying of the moon after going to the greatness of the fathers. The learned Brahmana wins these greatnesses of the sun and the moon, hence he obtains the greatness of Brahman, (yea) hence the greatness of Brahman. This is the Upanisat (mystic knowledge).

May that protect us both together, may that nourish us both together; may we show vigour together; may our learning be brilliant; may we not hate each other.

Om Peace! Peace! Peace! Harih Om.

  507. (Mantra 38) Mantra for washing the face after diving. ↩︎

  508. (Mantra 39) Mantra for diving again after washing. ↩︎

  509. (Mantra 40) Mantra for destroying the unknown sins. ↩︎

  510. (Mantra 41) Mantra to be uttered repeatedly after taking the bath. ↩︎

  511. (Mantras 42–47) Six Mantras for destroying the evils. ↩︎

  512. (Mantra 1) Mahāvyāhṛti-Mantras for Homa. ↩︎

  513. (Mantra 1) Mantra for obtaining abundant food. ↩︎

  514. (Mantra 1) Mantra for worship. ↩︎

  515. (Mantra 1) Mantra for removing the obstacles of knowledge. ↩︎

  516. (Mantra 1) Mantra to be repeated silently for obtaining the complete acquisition of the Veda. ↩︎

  517. (Mantra 1) Mantras for not forgetting the learned Vedas. ↩︎

  518. (Mantra 1) Austerity for liberation. ↩︎

  519. (Explanation) The numbers have been explained in three ways identifying the bull with the sacrifice, the sun and the inspired word as follows: ↩︎

  520. Mantra for tasting water before taking the meal. ↩︎

  521. Mantra offering five mouthfuls to the five Pranas before taking the meal. ↩︎

  522. Optional Mantra for the above. ↩︎

  523. Mantra for drinking the water after finishing the meal. ↩︎

  524. Mantra for rubbing the chest after the meal. ↩︎

  525. Five Mantras addressed to the five faces of Mahesvara (From Anuvaka XLIII to XLVII). ↩︎

  526. The series of three Mantras, named Trisuparna, to overcome the sins of killing Brahmanas, etc. (From the Anuvaka XLVIII to L). ↩︎

  527. Mantras for making a homa of eleven oblations for purification (From LI to LXI). ↩︎
